# Stock allocation fun

Movement-type keys use the shared SAP contract: a nonblank value must be exactly three numeric characters at orchestration, BAPI, audit, snapshot, and read-filter boundaries.

SAP-facing sales-order, reservation, and goods-movement document identities use the shared ten-character contract; short, nonnumeric, and all-zero keys are rejected before SAP reads, writes, persistence, or transaction side effects. Provider-created reservations, injected snapshots, persisted snapshots, and reservation result filters all use numeric, nonzero ten-character keys.

Goods-movement fiscal years use the shared four-character SAP contract and reject short, nonnumeric, or zero returned years before commit.

An ABAP stock allocation vertical slice for an existing SAP system.

The health report now exposes successful-run counts, run-quality rates, full/partial/unallocated demand-line counts and percentages, unit-safe core strategy quantities with explicit mixed-unit state, latest-run/requested-horizon/duration telemetry, actual selected-policy context and mixed-policy state, opt-in mixed-policy and mixed-unit warning state, stale-running threshold state, optional latest-duration, latest-completed-duration minimum/maximum, success-status requirement, consecutive-success-streak threshold, consecutive-non-success-streak threshold, minimum and maximum latest-completed allocated-line-count thresholds, maximum-average-duration, maximum-completed-duration, minimum-duration-sample-count, minimum-completion-rate, minimum-success-rate, minimum-success-count, maximum-error-rate, maximum-partial-run-rate, minimum-full-line-rate, maximum-unallocated-line-rate, maximum-partial-line-rate, minimum-full-line-count, maximum-demand-count, maximum-running-count, maximum-shortage-quantity warning thresholds, latest-completed coverage, shortage-percentage, shortage-quantity, allocated-quantity, available-stock, full-line-rate, partial-line-rate, full-line-count, deadline-age, and demand-count warning thresholds, and aggregated threshold-breach telemetry in its human, CSV, and JSON outputs; health machine-readable schema version is `115`.

The current health schema is `115` and supersedes earlier health schema references in the historical feature notes below. It includes successful-run counts, completion/success/partial/error rates, demand/full/partial/unallocated line counts and full/partial/unallocated line percentages, core strategy unit-safe quantities with explicit mixed-unit state, legacy-strategy telemetry, latest-run/requested-horizon/result/duration/running-age context, actual selected movement-type/shelf-life/safety-stock policy context with mixed-policy state, opt-in mixed-policy and mixed-unit warning state, stale-running threshold state, latest-, average-, maximum-completed-, and latest-completed-duration threshold state, latest-completed success-status, consecutive-success-streak, and consecutive-non-success-streak threshold state, minimum-duration-sample-count threshold state, coverage and shortage threshold values/state, completion-rate, success-rate, success-count, error-rate, partial-run-rate, full-line-rate, unallocated-line-rate, partial-line-rate, full-line-count, demand-count, running-count, shortage-quantity, latest-completed coverage minimum/maximum, latest-completed shortage percentage, latest-completed shortage quantity, latest-completed allocated-quantity minimum/maximum, latest-completed requested-quantity, latest-completed available-stock minimum/maximum, latest-completed full-line-rate minimum/maximum, latest-completed unallocated-line-rate, latest-completed partial-line-rate, latest-completed full-line-count, latest-completed unallocated-line-count, latest-completed shortage-line count, latest-completed allocated-line-count minimum/maximum, latest-completed deadline age, and latest-completed demand-count threshold state, and a deterministic threshold-breach count/list.

`p_lage` is an optional maximum latest-completed-age warning threshold in seconds; zero disables it. The report calculates `last_age_seconds` from the newest completed finish timestamp even when a newer audit is still running. JSON exposes unavailable age as `null`, CSV as `n/a`, and threshold state as `last_age_threshold_active`, `last_age_threshold`, and `last_age_above_threshold`. The completed result identity, timestamp, and allocation outcome are exposed as `last_completed_run_id`, `last_completed_status`, `last_completed_finish_date`, `last_completed_finish_time`, `last_completed_duration_seconds`, `last_completed_unit`, `last_completed_strategy`, `last_completed_requested`, `last_completed_allocated`, `last_completed_shortage`, `last_completed_coverage_pct`, `last_completed_demand_count`, `last_completed_full_line_count`, `last_completed_partial_line_count`, and `last_completed_unallocated_line_count`, with `last_completed_run_available` distinguishing an empty completed population. Normalized completed-run shortage and line rates use `last_completed_shortage_pct_available` and `last_completed_line_rates_available`; zero-request and zero-demand results remain explicitly unavailable with JSON `null` and CSV `n/a` values.
`last_age_reason` distinguishes `available`, `no_completed_run`, `invalid_timestamp`, and `future_timestamp` age states in JSON, CSV, and human output; the latter two remain neutral for `p_lage`.
`last_age_reference_date` and `last_age_reference_time` identify the exact report timestamp used to calculate `last_age_seconds`, including when the age is unavailable.
Latest-completed persisted available stock is exposed as `last_completed_available_stock_available`, `last_completed_available_stock`, and `last_completed_available_stock_unit`; zero is a valid available value, while unavailable context is JSON `null` and CSV `n/a`.
Latest-completed diagnostic text is exposed as `last_completed_message`, preserving persisted error or partial-run context separately from the latest run message.
Latest-completed start date/time are exposed as `last_completed_start_date` and `last_completed_start_time`, completing the persisted interval context alongside finish date/time and duration.
Latest-completed effective policy context is exposed through `last_completed_policy_available`, `last_completed_movement_type`, `last_completed_min_shelf_life`, and `last_completed_safety_stock`; unavailable no-run policy values are JSON `null` and CSV `n/a`.
Latest-completed requested-delivery context is exposed through `last_completed_horizon_available`, `last_completed_requested_on_from`, `last_completed_requested_on_to`, and `last_completed_requested_deadline`.
Latest-completed deadline age is exposed through `last_completed_deadline_age_available` and `last_completed_deadline_age_days`, calculated against `deadline_age_reference_date`; missing deadlines remain unavailable.

`p_cdag` is an optional maximum latest-completed deadline-age warning threshold in days; zero disables it, and completed runs without a requested deadline remain neutral. JSON, CSV, and human output expose `last_completed_deadline_age_threshold_active`, `last_completed_deadline_age_threshold`, and `last_completed_deadline_age_above_threshold`.

`p_cshmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed shortage-quantity warning threshold in the persisted result's unit; zero disables it, and a completed result with shortage above the threshold contributes `last_completed_shortage_quantity` to `threshold_breaches`. Completed coverage and shortage-percentage breaches are likewise surfaced as `last_completed_coverage` and `last_completed_shortage_pct`.
`p_camin` is an optional minimum latest-completed allocated-quantity warning threshold in the persisted result's unit; zero disables it, and completed results with no requested quantity remain neutral. A completed result whose allocated quantity is below the threshold contributes `last_completed_allocated` to `threshold_breaches`.
`p_crqmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed requested-quantity warning threshold in the persisted result's unit; zero disables it, and completed results with no requested quantity remain neutral. A completed result whose requested quantity is above the threshold contributes `last_completed_requested` to `threshold_breaches`.
`p_crqmin` is an optional minimum latest-completed requested-quantity warning threshold in the persisted result's unit; zero disables it, and completed results with no requested quantity remain neutral. A completed result whose requested quantity is below the threshold contributes `last_completed_requested_min` to `threshold_breaches`.
`p_cdmin` is an optional minimum latest-completed demand-count warning threshold; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A completed result whose demand count is below the threshold contributes `last_completed_demand_count_min` to `threshold_breaches`.
`p_caqmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed allocated-quantity warning threshold in the persisted result's unit; zero disables it, and completed results with no requested quantity remain neutral. A completed result whose allocated quantity is above the threshold contributes `last_completed_allocated_max` to `threshold_breaches`.

When both latest-completed allocated-quantity bounds are active, the report rejects a minimum above the maximum.

`p_ccvmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed coverage warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it, and completed results with no requested quantity remain neutral. A completed result whose coverage exceeds the threshold contributes `last_completed_coverage_max` to `threshold_breaches`. When both coverage bounds are active, the report rejects a minimum above the maximum.

`p_cflmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed full-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A completed result whose full-line rate exceeds the threshold contributes `last_completed_full_line_max` to `threshold_breaches`. When both full-line-rate bounds are active, the report rejects a minimum above the maximum.

`p_culcnt` is an optional maximum latest-completed unallocated-line-count warning threshold; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A completed result whose unallocated-line count exceeds the threshold contributes `last_completed_unallocated_count` to `threshold_breaches`.
`p_cplcnt` is an optional maximum latest-completed partial-line-count warning threshold; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A completed result whose partial-line count exceeds the threshold contributes `last_completed_partial_count` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_cshcnt` is an optional maximum latest-completed shortage-line-count warning threshold, calculated as partial plus unallocated lines; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A completed result whose shortage-line count exceeds the threshold contributes `last_completed_shortage_count` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_cdurmx` is an optional maximum latest-completed-duration warning threshold in seconds; zero disables it, and it evaluates the newest completed result even when a newer run is still running. A breach contributes `last_completed_duration` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_cdurmn` is an optional minimum latest-completed-duration warning threshold in seconds; zero disables it, and completed results with unavailable or zero duration remain neutral. A breach contributes `last_completed_duration_min` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_csucc` is an optional checkbox requiring the newest completed result to have status `S`; it remains neutral when no completed result exists. A partial or error newest completed result contributes `last_completed_not_success` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_cstrk` is an optional minimum consecutive-success streak across newest completed results; zero disables it, and no completed result remains neutral. A streak below the configured threshold contributes `last_completed_success_streak` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_cfail` is an optional maximum consecutive non-success streak across newest completed results; zero disables it, and no completed result remains neutral. A streak above the configured maximum contributes `last_completed_non_success_streak` to `threshold_breaches`, allowing a bounded tolerance for recent partial or error results.

`p_cacnt` is an optional minimum latest-completed allocated-line-count threshold, calculated as full plus partial lines; zero disables it, and a completed result with no demand lines remains neutral. A shortfall contributes `last_completed_allocated_count` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_cacmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed allocated-line-count threshold, using the same full-plus-partial calculation; zero disables it, and a completed result with no demand lines remains neutral. An excess contributes `last_completed_allocated_count_max` to `threshold_breaches`. When both bounds are active, the minimum cannot exceed the maximum.

`p_cavmin` and `p_cavmax` are optional minimum and maximum latest-completed available-stock warning thresholds in the persisted stock unit; zero disables each bound. They evaluate only when the completed result's stock context is available, so unavailable or mixed stock remains neutral; breaches contribute `last_completed_available_stock_min` or `last_completed_available_stock_max`.

`p_cflmin` is an optional minimum latest-completed full-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A breach contributes `last_completed_full_line` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_culmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed unallocated-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A breach contributes `last_completed_unallocated_line` to `threshold_breaches`.

`p_cplmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed partial-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it, and completed results with no demand lines remain neutral. A breach contributes `last_completed_partial_line` to `threshold_breaches`.

`last_completed_deadline_age_reason` identifies `available`, `no_deadline`, or `no_completed_run`, so an unavailable age is distinguishable from an empty completed population.

`p_cdmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed demand-count warning threshold; zero disables it, and an available completed result with demand count above the threshold contributes `last_completed_demand_count` to the breach list.

## SAP integration checklist

Import the ABAP objects under `src/` with abapGit into the package represented by `src/package.devc.xml` (the included package text is `$STOCK`); do not import `sap_stubs/`, the Node test harness, or generated `output/` files into SAP. The configured syntax baseline is SAP release 7.58, so the target system should be checked against that release before activation.

- Activate the client-dependent custom tables `ZSTOCKALLOC` and `ZSTOCKALLOC_RUN` from their included abapGit descriptors before running the reports. They are the only production database-write targets; standard SAP tables are read-only dependencies.
- Confirm the target system provides `MARA`, `MARC`, `MARD`, `MARM`, `MCHB`, `MCHA`, `VBAK`, `VBAP`, and `VBEP`, plus `MD_CONVERT_MATERIAL_UNIT`, `BAPI_RESERVATION_CREATE1`, `BAPI_RESERVATION_DELETE`, `BAPI_GOODSMVT_CREATE`, `BAPI_SALESORDER_CHANGE`, `BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT`, and `BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK`. The local `sap_stubs/` implementations model these dependencies for tests only.
- Assign read access (`S_TABU_NAM`, activity `03`) for the standard stock/order/unit tables and the custom audit/result tables. Assign custom-table activity `02` for audit/result writes and activity `06` for result replacement and retention deletes; reservation cancellation is governed by the `M_MRES_*` checks below and does not delete a custom table.
- Assign the operation-specific SAP authorizations used by the adapters: `M_MRES_BWA`/`M_MRES_WWA` activity `01` for reservation creation and activity `06` for cancellation, `M_MSEG_BWA`/`M_MSEG_WWA`/`M_MSEG_LGO` activity `01` for goods issue, and `V_VBAK_AAT` activity `02` for sales-order schedule-line changes. The adapters fail closed when these checks fail.
- Activate the reports `ZSTOCK_ALLOCATE`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_STOCK`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_CONVERT`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_RESERVE`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_RES_CANCEL`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_GOODS_ISSUE`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_ORDER_UPDATE`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_RESULT`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_HISTORY`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_WATCH`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_HEALTH`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_COMPARE`, and `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_PURGE`. Direct write reports require `P_EXEC`; `ZSTOCK_ALLOCATE` uses `P_TEST` for a no-write preview.
- Run the repository checks before transport (`npm test` and `npm run verify`) and execute a read-only stock/order smoke test in the target client before enabling write reports. The ABAP Unit harness validates the local contract; it does not replace SAP-side authorization, customizing, or business-data validation.

Current additions include adaptive strategy `A` (priority when all demand fits, fair-share when stock is scarce) and weighted fair-share strategy `W` (priority-weighted scarce-stock distribution), plus adaptive per-strategy analytics in history/result summaries and dedicated weighted metrics in audit, allocation, health, history, and result summaries. Current history summary schema is `43`, result summary schema is `46`; strategy filters accept `E` (fair-share), `A` (adaptive), and `W` (weighted fair-share). Result summaries expose the exact-run `adaptive_branch` as `priority`, `fair-share`, or `n/a` when originating audit context is unavailable or non-adaptive.

`ZSTOCK_ALLOC_STOCK` is a read-only operational report over the SAP stock source. It reads one material/plant/storage-location scope with an optional batch and exposes available quantity, canonical material unit, material/batch existence, batch-management and restriction flags, deletion-safe status, and batch expiration context in human, CSV, JSON, and typed JSON output. JSON and typed JSON use schema version `1`; typed JSON additionally uses numeric/boolean values for the availability fields, while CSV uses the same schema version and a stable one-row header.

`ZSTOCK_ALLOC_RES_CANCEL` is a guarded operational action for canceling one SAP reservation. It requires `p_exec`, accepts the reservation document, plant, and movement type, delegates validation/authorization/transaction handling to `ZCL_STOCK_RESERVATION_SAP`, and reports success or preserved BAPI/rollback diagnostics in human, CSV, JSON, and typed JSON output. Its JSON and CSV machine-readable schema version is `1`.

`ZSTOCK_ALLOC_RESERVE` is the guarded operational action for creating one SAP reservation outside the allocation run. It requires `p_exec`, accepts material/plant/storage-location, movement type, unit, quantity, required date, and optional batch, canonicalizes the unit, delegates validation/authorization/transaction handling to `ZCL_STOCK_RESERVATION_SAP`, and reports the returned reservation document or preserved BAPI/rollback diagnostics. Its machine-readable schema version is `1`.

`ZSTOCK_ALLOC_CONVERT` is a read-only diagnostic report for material-specific unit conversion. It accepts material, quantity, source unit, and target unit, canonicalizes both units, delegates conversion and authority checks to `ZCL_UNIT_CONVERSION_SAP`, and reports the source and converted quantities in human, CSV, JSON, and typed JSON output. Its JSON and CSV machine-readable schema version is `1`.

Fair-share allocators persist quantities to three decimal places. After proportional grants are rounded, any remaining representable `0.001` stock is redistributed deterministically without exceeding demand caps, so precision loss does not strand usable stock.

Current strategy contract: allocation, history, result, watch, health, purge, and comparison filters accept `P` (priority), `F` (FIFO), `N` (full-only), `S` (smallest), `L` (largest), `B` (best-fit), `E` (fair-share), `A` (adaptive), and `W` (weighted fair-share). Any older strategy-only examples below are superseded by this list.

Demand priorities are SAP delivery-priority values mapped to the internal range 0 through 99; malformed injected demand, allocator input, and persisted allocation snapshots outside that range are rejected before allocation or snapshot mutation.

Audit history reads and finalization require the full, partial, and unallocated outcome counts to sum to no more than the recorded demand count; inconsistent history rows fail validation instead of entering summaries or being persisted.

Allocation snapshot reads require a nonblank allocation unit on every persisted demand and its referenced run, so no result can be returned without quantity-unit provenance.

Adaptive allocation summaries and history summaries expose `adaptive_priority_runs`/`adaptive_fair_runs`, derived from each adaptive run's available-versus-requested quantities, so operators can see which branch the policy selected. Allocation success exports use schema `49`; history summary exports use schema `43`.

The current branch provides selectable deterministic priority, FIFO, full-only, smallest-demand-first, largest-demand-first, best-fit, fair-share, adaptive, and priority-weighted fair-share allocation with explicit full/partial/unallocated line status, preserved SAP sales-document/type/item/schedule-line keys, material-unit conversion for both demand and available stock, audited stock reads and conversions, audited allocation-calculation, snapshot-read, lock-acquisition, and audit-finalization failures, explicit material-existence and base-unit validation, SAP demand-unit validation, filtering of header- and item-delivery-blocked sales orders, rejection auditing for SAP demand-source validation failures, injectable SAP reservation and goods-movement authorization boundaries using `M_MRES_BWA`/`M_MRES_WWA` (reservation type/plant create/delete) and `M_MSEG_BWA`/`M_MSEG_WWA`/`M_MSEG_LGO` (goods-movement type/plant/storage) for both the allocation service and direct BAPI adapters, serialized allocation through an injected SAP enqueue/dequeue boundary, an explicit injectable allocation transaction boundary for replacement and retention writes, a report preview mode that calculates and audits outcomes without reservations or snapshot writes, rejection audit rows for pre-side-effect validation failures, unit-scoped audit history, strategy filtering and MCHB with required-batch, batch-existence, shelf-life, minimum remaining shelf-life, restricted-status, and delivery-date compatibility validation from MARA/MARC/MCHA, SAP-facing MARD/MARA/MARC and sales-order readers (VBAP/VBEP/VBAK), per-demand reservation posting through `BAPI_RESERVATION_CREATE1` plus `BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT` with preserved BAPI rejection text, explicit goods-issue and stock, queryable and explicitly retainable run-level audit history in `ZSTOCKALLOC_RUN` including the configured allocation unit and strategy, and a custom `ZSTOCKALLOC` result table. SAP-standard table and API test stubs are kept in `sap_stubs/`; application objects beginning with `Z` are kept in `src/`.

Every push and pull request runs the locked dependency install followed by `npm test` through the repository verification workflow. That gate covers abaplint, transpilation, ABAP Unit, report contracts, comparison contracts, and repository integration invariants.

`ZSTOCK_ALLOC_HEALTH` provides a read-only health view for a material/plant/storage-location scope. It classifies the scope as `HEALTHY`, `WARNING`, or `CRITICAL`, emits a stable `reason_code` (`NO_RUNS`, `ERROR_OR_STALE`, `THRESHOLD_BREACH`, `BACKLOG`, or `HEALTHY`), detects stale running work, reports core strategy counts and percentages plus aggregate demand/full/partial/unallocated line counts and full/partial/unallocated line percentages and unit-safe requested/allocated/shortage/coverage metrics for priority, FIFO, full-only, smallest, largest, and best-fit runs alongside fair-share, weighted fair-share, adaptive, and legacy metrics, accepts optional `p_strat` strategy scoping, `p_stat` audit-lifecycle scoping (`R`, `S`, `P`, or `E`), `p_msg` for a case-insensitive diagnostic-message substring, `p_monly` for nonblank diagnostics only, `p_dfrom`/`p_dto` for inclusive originating demand-count bounds, `p_avf`/`p_avt` for inclusive persisted available-stock bounds, `p_qf`/`p_qt` for inclusive persisted requested-quantity bounds, `p_af`/`p_at` for inclusive persisted allocated-quantity bounds, `p_shf`/`p_sht` for inclusive persisted shortage-quantity bounds, `p_spf`/`p_spt` for inclusive persisted shortage-percentage bounds from 0 through 100, `p_covf`/`p_covt` for inclusive coverage-percentage bounds from 0 through 100, `p_tfrom`/`p_tto` for nonnegative inclusive completed-audit-duration bounds in seconds, `p_from`/`p_to` for an inclusive persisted audit start-date window, `p_ffrom`/`p_fto` for an inclusive persisted audit finish-date window, and `p_age_to` for a nonnegative maximum running-audit age in seconds. `p_durmax` is an optional nonnegative latest-run duration warning threshold in seconds; zero disables it and running or unfinished latest runs do not breach it. `p_cdurmx` and `p_cdurmn` are optional nonnegative maximum and minimum latest-completed-duration warning thresholds in seconds; zero disables each, and unavailable or zero completed durations remain neutral for the minimum threshold. `p_avgmax` is an optional nonnegative average completed-duration warning threshold in seconds; zero disables it and a population with no completed duration metrics does not breach it. `p_maxdur` is an optional nonnegative maximum completed-duration warning threshold in seconds; zero disables it and a population with no completed duration metrics does not breach it. `p_durcnt` is an optional nonnegative minimum completed-duration sample-count warning threshold; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_runcnt` is an optional nonnegative minimum total-run-count warning threshold; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_dcmin` is an optional nonnegative minimum deadline-bearing-run-count warning threshold; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_pmix` is an optional checkbox that warns when the selected population mixes movement type, minimum shelf life, or safety-stock policy; it is disabled by default. `p_umix` is an optional checkbox that warns when the selected population mixes allocation units; it is disabled by default. `p_cmin` is an optional minimum completion-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_succ` is an optional minimum success-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_sucnt` is an optional minimum successful-run-count warning threshold as a nonnegative count; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_errmax` is an optional maximum error-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_prtmax` is an optional maximum partial-run-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected population with no runs does not breach it. `p_flmin` is an optional minimum full-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected population with no demand lines does not breach it. `p_ulmax` is an optional maximum unallocated-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected population with no demand lines does not breach it. `p_cplmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed partial-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected completed result with no demand lines does not breach it. `p_plmax` is an optional maximum partial-line-rate warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it and a selected population with no demand lines does not breach it. `p_flcnt` is an optional minimum full-line-count warning threshold as a nonnegative count; zero disables it and a selected population with no demand lines does not breach it. `p_dmax` is an optional maximum demand-count warning threshold as a nonnegative count; zero disables it and a selected population at or below the threshold does not breach it. `p_rmax` is an optional maximum running-count warning threshold as a nonnegative count; zero disables it and a selected population at or below the threshold does not breach it. `p_shmax` is an optional maximum shortage-quantity warning threshold in the comparable selected unit; zero disables it and mixed-unit populations never breach it. `p_avmin` and `p_avmax` are optional nonnegative minimum and maximum available-stock warning thresholds for the selected persisted baseline; zero disables each bound, empty populations do not breach, and mixed-value or mixed-unit populations remain neutral because their available-stock context is unavailable. `p_stale` is an optional nonnegative stale-running age threshold in seconds; zero disables stale filtering and stale threshold telemetry, while a positive value marks matching running work as critical. Running audits are excluded when a duration or finish-date bound is supplied; the maximum running age is applied alongside the `p_stale` lower threshold and must not be below it when both are active. These filters can be combined with `p_legacy` to select blank-strategy legacy runs and `p_ovrd` to retain only runs whose effective requested deadline (`requested_on_to`, falling back to `requested_on_from`) is before the selected as-of date. `p_odate` supplies that overdue as-of date and defaults to the SAP system date when overdue filtering is enabled; it requires `p_ovrd`. `p_dead` retains only runs with an effective requested deadline, while `p_deadf`/`p_deadt` select an inclusive effective deadline range; `p_dagef`/`p_daget` select an inclusive signed deadline-age range where positive values are overdue days and negative values are days remaining, and `p_daged` overrides the age reference date (otherwise the SAP system date is used). Age bounds require a valid range and `p_daged` requires an age bound; these filters can be combined with overdue filtering. The report also accepts `p_cov` as a minimum coverage warning threshold, `p_spct` as a maximum shortage-percentage warning threshold, `p_avgmax` as a maximum average-duration warning threshold, `p_crqmin` as a minimum latest-completed requested-quantity warning threshold, `p_crqmax` as a maximum latest-completed requested-quantity warning threshold, `p_caqmax` as a maximum latest-completed allocated-quantity warning threshold, and `p_plmax` as a maximum partial-line-rate warning threshold, `p_flcnt` as a minimum full-line-count warning threshold, `p_dmax` as a maximum demand-count warning threshold, `p_rmax` as a maximum running-count warning threshold, and `p_shmax` as a maximum shortage-quantity warning threshold, `p_avmin` as a minimum available-stock warning threshold, and `p_avmax` as a maximum available-stock warning threshold (`0` disables each threshold); `p_spf`/`p_spt`, `p_covf`/`p_covt`, `p_tfrom`/`p_tto`, `p_from`/`p_to`, `p_ffrom`/`p_fto`, and `p_age_to` are independent population bounds. Reversed or invalid duration, lifecycle-date, running-age, latest-completed requested-quantity, or latest-completed demand-count ranges are rejected. `p_legacy` cannot be combined with `p_strat`. Configured `coverage_threshold` and `shortage_threshold` values are exported alongside their active/breached state. Breaches are exposed as `coverage_below_threshold`, `shortage_above_threshold`, `average_duration_above_threshold`, `maximum_duration_above_threshold`, `completion_below_threshold`, `success_below_threshold`, `success_count_below_threshold`, `duration_count_below_threshold`, `run_count_below_threshold`, `deadline_count_below_threshold`, `mixed_policy_breach`, `mixed_unit_breach`, `error_above_threshold`, `partial_above_threshold`, `full_line_below_threshold`, `unallocated_line_above_threshold`, `partial_line_above_threshold`, `full_count_below_threshold`, `demand_count_above_threshold`, and `shortage_quantity_above_threshold`, `available_stock_below_threshold`, `available_stock_above_threshold`, `running_count_above_threshold`, and `stale_above_threshold` and `last_completed_deadline_age_above_threshold` in JSON/CSV; `threshold_breach_count` and deterministic pipe-delimited `threshold_breaches` (`coverage`, `shortage`, `duration`, `average_duration`, `maximum_duration`, `last_completed_duration`, `last_completed_duration_min`, `last_completed_not_success`, `duration_count`, `run_count`, `deadline_count`, `completion`, `success`, `success_count`, `error`, `partial`, `full_line`, `unallocated_line`, `partial_line`, `full_count`, `demand_count`, `shortage_quantity`, `last_completed_allocated`, `last_completed_requested`, `last_completed_requested_min`, `last_completed_demand_count_min`, `last_completed_allocated_max`, `last_completed_coverage_max`, `last_completed_full_line_max`, `last_completed_unallocated_count`, `last_completed_partial_count`, `last_completed_shortage_count`, `last_shortage_pct`, `available_stock_min`, `available_stock_max`, `mixed_policies`, `mixed_units`, `running_count`, `last_completed_deadline_age`) summarize all simultaneous breaches and change the status to `WARNING` unless errors or stale work make it `CRITICAL`. Health JSON/CSV exports use schema `115` and expose selected scope and policy/filter provenance including `material`, `plant`, `storage_location`, `batch`, `movement_type_filter`, `unit_filter`, `minimum_shelf_life_filter`, `safety_stock_filter`, `minimum_safety_stock_filter`, `maximum_safety_stock_filter`, `requested_on_from_filter`, `requested_on_to_filter`, `status_filter`, `preview_filter`, `message_filter`, `message_only`, `minimum_demand_count`, `maximum_demand_count`, `minimum_available_stock`, `maximum_available_stock`, `minimum_requested_quantity`, `maximum_available_stock_threshold`, `stale_threshold_seconds`, `policy_context_available`, `mixed_policies`, `movement_type_context`, `minimum_shelf_life_context`, `safety_stock_context`, `mixed_units`, `last_requested_on_from`, `last_requested_on_to`, and `last_requested_deadline`, `warn_mixed_policies`, `mixed_policy_warning_active`, `mixed_policy_breach`, `run_count_threshold_active`, `run_count_threshold`, `run_count_below_threshold`, `deadline_count_threshold_active`, `deadline_count_threshold`, `deadline_count_below_threshold`, `warn_mixed_units`, `mixed_unit_warning_active`, and `mixed_unit_breach`, plus the selected population's `deadline_count`, earliest/latest effective deadlines, signed last/oldest/newest deadline ages, and `deadline_age_reference_date`, plus `available_stock_context_available`, `available_stock_context`, and `mixed_available_stock`, `available_stock_min_threshold`, `available_stock_below_threshold`, `available_stock_max_threshold`, and `available_stock_above_threshold` so a consistent persisted baseline is distinguished from mixed or unavailable stock context; mixed-unit quantities remain unavailable rather than being summed.

Health JSON errors use schema `115`, matching the successful health envelope.

Health recency telemetry includes `last_available_stock_available`, `last_available_stock`, and `last_available_stock_unit` for the selected latest run. The availability flag distinguishes a persisted zero from an unavailable baseline; unavailable values are emitted as JSON `null` and CSV `n/a`.

Latest-run result telemetry includes `last_requested_quantity`, `last_allocated_quantity`, `last_shortage_quantity`, `last_coverage_pct`, `last_demand_count`, `last_full_line_count`, `last_partial_line_count`, and `last_unallocated_line_count`. These fields describe the same persisted run as `last_run_id`; they are emitted as JSON `null` and CSV `n/a` when no selected run exists.

`p_lcov` is an optional minimum latest-run coverage warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it, and a selected latest run with no requested quantity remains neutral. Its active value and breach state are exported as `last_coverage_threshold_active`, `last_coverage_threshold`, and `last_coverage_below_threshold`; a breach contributes `last_coverage` to `threshold_breaches` and changes a healthy result to `WARNING`.

`p_lshmax` is an optional maximum latest-run shortage-quantity warning threshold in the selected latest run's persisted unit; zero disables it, and a selected latest run with no requested quantity remains neutral. Its active value and breach state are exported as `last_shortage_quantity_threshold_active`, `last_shortage_quantity_threshold`, and `last_shortage_quantity_above_threshold`; a breach contributes `last_shortage_quantity` to `threshold_breaches` and changes a healthy result to `WARNING`.

Latest-run line-rate telemetry includes `last_line_rates_available`, `last_full_line_pct`, `last_partial_line_pct`, and `last_unallocated_line_pct`. It is available only when the latest persisted run has a positive demand count; otherwise the rates are JSON `null` and CSV `n/a`.

Latest-run shortage-rate telemetry includes `last_shortage_pct_available` and `last_shortage_pct`. It is available only when the latest persisted run has a positive requested quantity; otherwise the rate is JSON `null` and CSV `n/a`.

`p_lspct` is an optional maximum latest-run shortage-percentage warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent; zero disables it, and a latest run with no requested quantity remains neutral. Its active value and breach state are exported as `last_shortage_pct_threshold_active`, `last_shortage_pct_threshold`, and `last_shortage_pct_above_threshold`; a breach contributes `last_shortage_pct` to `threshold_breaches` and changes a healthy result to `WARNING`.

`p_ccov` is an optional minimum latest-completed coverage warning threshold from 0 through 100 percent, `p_ccvmax` is an optional maximum latest-completed coverage warning threshold in the same range, and `p_cspct` is an optional maximum latest-completed shortage-percentage threshold in the same range. All are zero-disabled and remain neutral when no completed run has a positive requested quantity. Their configured values and breach states are exported as `last_completed_coverage_threshold`, `last_completed_coverage_below_threshold`, `last_completed_coverage_max_threshold`, `last_completed_coverage_above_threshold`, `last_completed_shortage_pct_threshold`, and `last_completed_shortage_pct_above_threshold`; completed-quality breaches contribute `last_completed_coverage`, `last_completed_coverage_max`, or `last_completed_shortage_pct` to `threshold_breaches`.

The service validates injected provider and allocator postconditions before side effects, preserves demand identity and source metadata, rejects inconsistent or stale snapshot reservation metadata, preflights cancellation authority for legacy reservations, and persists replacement snapshots before canceling superseded reservations. Cleanup uncertainty is recorded as a partial run with the actionable diagnostic; failed persistence leaves the prior snapshot/reservation pair untouched.

The SAP stock adapter requires a MARC plant-data row when the material exists, treats a batch master present in `MCHA` but without a storage-stock row in `MCHB` as a valid zero-stock batch, and rejects material, plant-material, storage-stock, batch-stock, and batch-master records marked for deletion (`LVORM = X`) rather than allocating from data pending removal.

The SAP stock adapter also rejects noncanonical values in the boolean `MARA-XCHPF` batch-management and `MCHA-ZUSTD` batch-restriction indicators instead of treating corrupt status data as false.

SAP batch-expiration (`MCHA-VFDAT`) and requested-delivery (`VBEP-EDATU`) dates are validated as calendar dates before shelf-life, delivery compatibility, or horizon filtering; malformed provider dates fail closed.

Deletion indicators from `MARD`, `MCHB`, `MARA`, `MARC`, and `MCHA` must likewise be blank or `X`; malformed `LVORM` values are rejected at the stock-source boundary. `MARC-LVORM` protects the selected material/plant relation from allocation when it is flagged for deletion.

The direct SAP operator reports (`ZSTOCK_ALLOC_STOCK`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_CONVERT`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_RESERVE`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_RES_CANCEL`, `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_GOODS_ISSUE`, and `ZSTOCK_ALLOC_ORDER_UPDATE`) publish `schema_version: 1` in both typed and untyped JSON success envelopes and in JSON error envelopes, while retaining their stable CSV machine-readable contracts.

The SAP order reader validates canonical `VBAP-LOEKZ` deletion flags, then excludes rejected, deleted (`VBAP-LOEKZ`), header-delivery-blocked (`VBAK-LIFSK`), item-delivery-blocked (`VBAP-LIFSP`), and schedule-line-delivery-blocked (`VBEP-LIFSP`) sales-order items before mapping open schedule-line demand.

The SAP order reader also rejects negative requested or confirmed schedule quantities, including rows whose difference would otherwise appear positive.

Snapshot persistence and reads verify that the supplied run ID exists in `ZSTOCKALLOC_RUN`, has a recognized lifecycle status and strategy (while preserving blank legacy strategy rows), belongs to the same material/plant/storage/batch/unit scope,
